Acceptance: sample the genuine mismatches, compare the precompute scalars vs ground truth, determine which side fails.

    **Process (subagent-first, parallel orchestration).** A read-only Explore agent mapped the combat-verdict pipeline (`agents/daemon_slayer/matchup.py _classify` 4-band, threshold `_TRADE_MARGIN=0.10`; the reader `core/precomputed_laning_coach.laning_band` 5-band recalibration `_HOLD_LOW=0.05`/`_BACK_OFF=0.18`; `tools/hz_shadow_report.classify_verdict`; and the prime structural suspect: the enemy is always modeled at FULL combo, `sequence_b=_FULL_COMBO`, `core/laning_scenario_precompute.py:355`). A Plan subagent emitted the file:line-grounded spec - KEY FINDING: the shadow log does NOT store `net_swing`, but `choices[0].expected_outcome` LITERALLY embeds it ("net swing -0.12; you remove 30% of X, they remove 42% of you"), so the diagnosis parses the LOG-TIME scalar (zero engine drift) instead of re-deriving from the live 1.149.0 table. A background build agent implemented it TDD-first; an independent read-only `verifier` subagent gate confirmed green (22/22, ruff clean, partition invariant, no top-level core/agents import, hermetic) BEFORE this commit.

    **Built.** `tools/hz_mismatch_diagnose.py` - a standalone CLI (tools/ sys.path safe; reuses `hz_shadow_report.record_agreement` so the mismatch classes are byte-identical to the ~55% agreement gate, partition invariant proven 945 == 945 == (2103-1158)); parses each genuine-mismatch tick's log-time `net_swing`, buckets it (deep-neg / firm back_off / hold-zone / even / favored-trade), and emits a mechanical CALIBRATION-vs-MODEL-ERROR verdict per class; writes `ops/audit/HZ_MISMATCH_DIAGNOSE.md` with an anti-circularity footer. +22 synthetic TDD tests, ruff clean.

    **THE DIAGNOSIS (real log, 17409 records, 945 genuine laning mismatches).** The over-caution is MOSTLY CALIBRATION, NOT Haiku noise: `hold->all_in` (n=151, median -0.02), `even->trade` (n=126, median -0.06) and `hold->trade` (n=91, median +0.06) all cluster their net_swing right at the hold/trade boundary = the precompute is conservative within a defensible band (368 mismatches). ONE structural MODEL-ERROR pocket: `back_off->trade` (n=138, median -0.30, mass in firm/deep back_off) = the precompute over-states incoming damage because it scores the enemy landing their FULL combo (`sequence_b=_FULL_COMBO`) - a threshold tune cannot fix a structural-input error, the enemy-sequence model is the lever. The LARGEST class `back_off->hold` (n=351, median -0.24) reads MIXED - firm-negative but neither deep enough for clear model-error nor near the threshold for clean calibration, genuinely per-pair ambiguous. Caveat: 6972/17409 records were logged under a non-current engine; the buckets reflect log-time precompute output (the correct denominator for "was it too cautious WHEN it spoke").

    **NEXT (the flip blocker, all operator-gated).** The diagnosis converts the abstract 54.6% agreement gate into 3 actionable levers: (i) a realistic PARTIAL enemy-combo model (`sequence_b`) is the principled MODEL-ERROR fix for `back_off->trade` - NOT a threshold tune; (ii) the CALIBRATION classes justify INVESTIGATING a `_BACK_OFF`/`_HOLD_LOW`/`_TRADE` nudge ONLY WITH ground-truth corroboration (the optional rewind-db trade-outcome cross-ref, left a one-line follow-up in the tool, or a live side-by-side) - never to chase the metric (anti-circularity); (iii) `back_off->hold` needs per-pair inspection. The flip stays do-not-flip-blind.

    **Parallel overlay track (operator directive; a live SR Caitlyn game was UP).** Did the ROADMAP-mandated per-cycle live overlay verification: 25/25 overlay snapshot tests pass (the lone teardown ERROR is the live game writing `data/*.jsonl`, the documented environmental guard, not a failure); a read-only audit confirmed `w-spike` (20,580) / `w-trinket` (20,520) default positions (`web/js/lib/overlay_layout.js:50-51`) are a clean left-edge column with NO collision (Gemini's suggested tune is a non-issue); a live composite screenshot confirmed CALL + WARD-UP rendering in clean doctrine positions, click-through, no HUD/play-area collision; and a process-vs-mtime check proved the RUNNING overlay (electron pid 9736 started 19:32:51) post-dates the ZOI render files (`minimap_zoi.js` mtime 19:30:33), so the operator is on FRESH ZOI code, not stale. 574. DONE **2026-06-21 (headless continue 8). HZ shadow flip-readiness metric DE-BIASED - native "recall" actions were a CATEGORY ERROR deflating the laning agreement gate for the #1 Haiku-to-ZERO north star. Commit `79a7906a` (pushed, upstream tracking restored after the item-569 history rewrite dropped it). Tier-1 tooling; no engine / DS / Share / ENGINE_VERSION change.**

    **Triage (candidates 1+2 verified DRAINED before building - the curated backlog had 3 already-shipped picks in a row, so grep-verify was mandatory).** Candidate (1) DS comprehensive cross-eval Tier-2 next pass: the concrete Aphelios dps zero-output BUG (cluster C) is ALREADY FIXED (`ops/audit/ds_cross_eval/SYSTEMIC_FINDINGS.md:74-84` + `:112` mark it DONE at ENGINE 1.128.0) and LIVE-CONFIRMED this session (re-probed via `tools/ds_cross_eval/probe_champion.py Aphelios` at live ENGINE 1.149.0 -> dps scorer ranks Yun Tal 103 / IE 96 / Stormrazor 82 / Kraken 70 with real non-zero scores, no longer collapsed to Doran's). Cluster B1 melee-applicability gate SHIPPED default-OFF (item 496); F2 gold-aware top SHIPPED default-OFF (item 497); Cluster A ARAM archetype-override = a per-champion off-meta operator decision (only Shaco a clean CONFIRM), NOT a bounded build. So (1) has no bounded buildable slice left. Candidate (2) HZ flip-readiness: ran `tools/hz_shadow_report.py` - laning agreement 1133/3862=29.3%, build 199/407=48.9%, report says do-not-flip (operator gate); the flip is NOT ready. The 5-day-old memory `project_ds_comprehensive_cross_eval` (Aphelios "the clearest true defect") predated the 1.128.0 fix - the same stale-backlog trap as items 1/b/c.

    **Gemini director consult** (`tools/gemini_ask.ps1 -Model gemini-3-pro-preview`): given (1) drained and (2) not flip-ready, confirmed the most valuable bounded slice = fix the HZ shadow agreement metric's recall category error (an honest flip-readiness gate is a hard prerequisite for the Haiku-to-ZERO flip). Acceptance: native recall scored against the precompute ECONOMY block (or excluded from the laning-combat denominator); honest agreement %.

    **Root cause (spec via a Plan subagent, verified file:line; impl TDD-inline; read-only verifier gate before commit).** `tools/hz_shadow_report.summarize_agreement` scored the precompute's choice-A laning-COMBAT verdict (trade/hold/back_off/all_in/even, from `core/precomputed_laning_coach._VERDICT_LABELS`) against the native Haiku action even when that action classified to "recall" - an ECONOMY decision the combat-A can STRUCTURALLY never be (the precompute's recall signal is choice B: "Recall now"/"Back soon" from `_RECALL_LABELS`). Every native-recall tick was a guaranteed mismatch (live: `hold->recall x1143` + `back_off->recall x649` = ~1794 ticks) dragging laning agreement to 29.3% and masking the true precompute-vs-Haiku combat signal.

    **Fix.** `record_agreement` returns None when native=="recall" (excluded from the laning-combat comparable); `summarize_agreement` routes those ticks to a NEW `economy` sub-block `{native_recall, precompute_also_recall}` - native recall scored against the precompute economy block via `_precompute_offers_recall` (ANY choice label is a recall directive). New `_is_recall_directive_label` treats BOTH "Recall now" (classify_verdict hit) AND "Back soon" (which classify_verdict MISSES - no keyword) as recall, via a hardcoded `frozenset({"recall now","back soon"})` mirroring `_RECALL_LABELS`. Hardcoded NOT imported: a top-level `from core ...` import breaks the standalone `python tools/hz_shadow_report.py` CLI (verified live: `tools/` is on sys.path, not the repo root -> ModuleNotFoundError). Build (lean-axis) agreement and the even<->hold mapping are untouched.

    **Result (live + tests).** Laning agreement 29.3% -> **54.6%** (1133/2076); the 1794 native-recall ticks now show in the economy block (all 1794 with the precompute economy also recommending a back); combined flip hint 31% -> 54% over 2483 comparable ticks; remaining laning mismatches are now GENUINE combat disagreements (back_off->hold x351, hold->all_in x151, the actionable gate). TDD: +6 new tests, 3 updated (`tests/test_hz_shadow_report.py`), 41/41 pass; ruff clean; py_compile OK. Read-only `verifier` subagent re-ran independently (117/117 across `test_hz_shadow_report.py` + `test_precomputed_laning_coach.py`, confirmed the fix in-impl + no production importer). The flip itself stays operator-gated (do-not-flip-blind). 571. DONE **2026-06-21 (headless continue 5). ZOI overlay item 567 SLICE 3 - team-colored Zone-of-Influence bubbles + a demarcation line render INSIDE the w-mmrect click-through box, weighted by RC per-team strength, and a deterministic map-control callout feeds the coach. Commit `163b76ee` (code) + docs sync; pushed. Tier-1 frontend + 1 new pure core module + dashboard wiring + tests; NO engine / DS / Share / ENGINE_VERSION bump.**

    **Process (subagent-first).** Gemini director consult (`tools/gemini_ask.ps1`) for slice-3 intent/acceptance -> a Plan subagent emitted the file:line-grounded spec + a FROZEN `/api/state.zoi` contract -> 2 build agents on DISJOINT file sets (backend: core + `_state_builder.py` + ui_mock json; frontend: `minimap_zoi.js` + `main.js` + html/css + snapshot test) coded to the contract -> a read-only verifier gate caught a real commit-blocker (245x U+2500 box-drawing chars in the JS comment banners - an ASCII hard-rule violation the agents' "green" missed), fixed before commit.

    **Backend** (`core/zoi_influence.py`, pure / stateless / no-numpy). `compute_zoi(dots, my_level, game_time_s) -> {bubbles, demarcation, map_control}` in BOX-FRACTION [0,1] (resolution-independent). Per-dot `r_frac = clamp(15 + (px*conf*0.1)*power*(1+T), 15, 60)/312`; ally power +0.15 per crossed ult spike (6/11/16), enemy power `1 + T*0.2` (`T=game_time/1200`). `demarcation` = weighted perpendicular bisector of the two team weighted-centroids, clipped to the box edges (None when one team is absent). `map_control` = `ally_control_pct` (presence-weight ratio) + `action_quadrant` + a templated line. `zoi_callout` emits the canonical `{tag,line,eta_s,kind}` dict appended to `det["callouts"]` (the no-Haiku coaching surface) in `_state_builder.py`. HONEST SCOPE: strength is PRESENCE-grounded (dot px*confidence + MY ult spikes + game_time) - the Live Client exposes no enemy levels / alive-dead / gold, so there is no true gold-lead/alive input; `blue=ally / red=enemy` assumes League default minimap colors. 18 unit tests (empty / one-team / NaN-safety / spike-bump / radius-clamp / control-pct-bounds / callout-shape / ascii / demarc-clipped).

    **Frontend** (`web/js/panels/minimap_zoi.js`, HTML canvas immediate-mode, GPU-light) child of `#am-mmrect`. `globalAlpha` hard-capped 0.25; the key fix = PER-TEAM OFFSCREEN COMPOSITING - each team's bubbles (+ the ally-side tint, folded into the blue layer) are flattened in an offscreen buffer then blitted at the cap, so overlapping same-team bubbles can NEVER stack past 0.25 (a live CDP pixel sample caught raw on-canvas overlap compositing to alpha 0.467; post-fix 99.7% of painted pixels are <= 0.25, peak 0.349 only in the 0.27% contested blue/red seam). EMA-smoothed (~1s settle) anti-jitter; `pointer-events:none` on canvas + parent so it NEVER eats a minimap move/ping/cast click; null/off-shell -> clear. `zoi` threaded into client `state.latest` at all 3 `main.js` poll sites + 2 ui_mock sites; ui_mock fixture `zoi` block + an overlay threading snapshot test added. 18 `node --test`.

    **LIVE-VERIFIED** (operator mid-SR game). RC restarted -> `/api/state.zoi` = 29 bubbles + finite demarcation + `map_control` 60-91% ally (dominant game), and the map-control callout appears in `/api/state.callouts` (coach feed live). rc-shell RELAUNCHED (no hot-reload of new overlay JS) + CDP DOM/pixel inspect of `#am-zoi-canvas`: 312x312 backing store stretched to the 411px box, inside the gold frame, painting blue-dominant pixels over the real minimap, `pointer-events:none` on both. 563. DONE **2026-06-21 (gemini+AHK headless DIRECTOR REFILL loop, cycle R12 - ds-sweep). DS schema lift: cross-spell all-source TARGET-VULNERABILITY mark registry, the cross-source sibling of the per-spell self-amp registry. Engine commit `cad49029` (inline sole-orchestrator slice, tightly-coupled); Tier-2 (ENGINE 1.148.0 -> 1.149.0, DS :8893 restarted -> 1.149.0 live, Share synced 370 files, --check green). Read-only verifier subagent CONFIRM gate before commit (7/7 claims).**

    **What.** NEW `agents/daemon_slayer/_target_vulnerability_overrides.py` models an all-source vulnerability MARK - a debuff the wielder lays on the TARGET that makes the marked target take +X% damage FROM ALL SOURCES (autos, abilities, item procs, allies). This is the all-source half that the per-spell self-amp `_ability_amp_overrides.py` (which amps only the caster's OWN one spell) can never express. `TargetVulnEntry` (amp, source_key, kind, note) + two registries by source kind: `_CHAMPION_VULN_OVERRIDES` (champion_id -> ABILITY mark) and `_ITEM_VULN_OVERRIDES` (item-id str -> ITEM-passive mark). `target_vuln_multiplier(champion_id, item_ids)` composes the product of `(1+amp)` over every mark the wielder owns (multiplicative per independent amp source; a unique item id counted at most once via a de-dup guard).

    **Seeded (verified vs 16.12.1 ground truth).** 2 ACTIVE: Vladimir R Hemoplague - DDragon `champion_detail/Vladimir.json` tooltip "take {{e2}}% increased damage from all sources", `effect[2]=[10,10,10]` -> 0.10 rank-flat; Evenshroud `3001` + Arena 22-mirror `223001` - `items.json` "Coruscation: After Immobilizing ... take 7% increased damage for 5 seconds" -> 0.07 (both ids, the Arena mirror verified identical).

    **Ground-truth deviation (logged, not silent).** The director's directive named Imperial Mandate `4005` at 6%, but patch 16.12.1 `items_meraki.json` "Coordinated Fire" is a current-HP mark-DETONATION (allies that damage a marked enemy consume the mark for 10% of the target's CURRENT HEALTH as bonus magic damage, 9s/target CD), NOT a +X% increased-damage-from-all-sources multiplier. No 6% all-source value exists in the data to characterize against Meraki ground truth (the directive's own "using values from the current patch" + "TDD vs Meraki ground truth" clauses), so 4005 is recorded in `_NONFIT_VULN_CANDIDATES` (documented with its real mechanic, NOT seeded) rather than modeled as a fiction - a WRONG precompute is worse than none; it belongs in an ally-detonation seam (cf. allyamp.py), not this all-source-%amp registry. Mirrors the established `_ability_amp_overrides` INERT-with-reason / `_STAGED_AMP_CANDIDATES` doctrine.

    **Seam.** `dps.compute_dps` gains `apply_target_vuln: bool = False` (appended at END per Python conventions). When ON, `weighted_dps` + every `phase_dps` is multiplied by the composed mark multiplier (scaling the wielder's whole DPS output - autos + procs + the routed on_hit passive); when OFF (default) the multiplier is never resolved and the AA-DPS path is byte-identical. Models the fully-marked target at full magnitude (the `assume_takedown` / `assume_ability_amp` developed-fight doctrine); uptime gating (Vlad R cooldown, Evenshroud's 5s post-immobilize window) is a future live-consumer concern, not baked here.

    **Verification.** TDD RED-first (23 R12 tests: registry shape, ground-truth values, resolver composition/dedup/int-normalization, byte-identical-OFF, x1.10 on-path, phase_dps scaled, non-fit guard, ASCII). Read-only verifier subagent CONFIRM (7/7: files exist, 23/23 fresh, ENGINE 1.149.0, zero quoted "1.148.0" remaining under agents/daemon_slayer, BYTE_IDENTICAL_OK, VULN_APPLIES_OK x1.10, NONFIT_OK). ENGINE bump = quoted-literal-only across 83 DS files (93 replacements; bare-prose comments untouched per the item-421 regress trap). DS suite **7459 passed / 1 skipped / 1942 subtests**; ruff clean. Live default-ON flip + broadening the consumer beyond AA-DPS to ability_dps/burst EXCLUDED -> `docs/LIVE_GAME_GATED_SYNC.md`. Built as ONE worktree slice + read-only verifier CONFIRM gate before the --no-ff merge.**

    **What.** NEW `agents/daemon_slayer/_passive_flat_mitigation_overrides.py` models the PER-INSTANCE FLAT-AMOUNT damage-reduction class that the percent `_passive_mitigation_overrides.py` docstring (lines 64-69) explicitly EXCLUDED ("reduces a flat number per hit, OR caps at a % of EACH instance -> hit-count / instance-size dependent"). Seeded 3 from `data/daemon_slayer/16.12.1/champion_abilities.json` verbatim: Fizz P (flat 4, ANY axis, innate prob 1.0; the +1% AP sub-term omitted - no AP ctx on this EHP seam), Amumu E (per-rank [5,7,9,11,13], PHYS, prob 1.0), Leona W (per-rank [8,12,16,20,24], ANY, short active amortized at prob 0.3). All cap_frac 0.5 ("up to 50% of the instance"), non-binding at representative champion-combat instance sizes (flat 4-24 << 50% of a 100-300 final-damage hit) so the flat amount governs.

    **Model (EHP NUMERATOR add).** Default-OFF `assume_passive_flat_mitigation` seam on `compute_ehp` + `rank_items_by_ehp`. `flat_mitigation_hp(champ, level, flag)` returns per-axis prevented-damage HP = `_ASSUMED_FLAT_DR_INSTANCES`(=6, the representative mitigated-instance count over a fight - the live per-instance feed we lack, analog of the percent registry's `_ACTIVE_DR_PROB`) x per-instance-flat x conditional_probability. Folded into the three EHP numerators exactly like `ext_flat_hp` (ehp.py:1181-1183): prevented post-mitigation damage behaves like bonus max-HP, riding the same armor/MR curve. `_ASSUMED_ABILITY_RANK`=4 reads the per-rank Amumu/Leona blocks. Byte-identical when OFF -> all three returns 0.0.

    **Deviation (followed the data).** The directive assumed a per-LEVEL flat curve; 16.12.1 shows Amumu E + Leona W carry per-RANK (5 ability-rank) flat blocks, not an 18-level curve. Modeled rank_scaled with `_ASSUMED_ABILITY_RANK`. Fizz P is a flat innate 4.

    **Verification.** TDD (RED-first) `test_passive_flat_mitigation_overrides_r9.py`. Proven byte-identical OFF: Amumu blended_ehp 2401.33 == 2401.33; `flat_mitigation_hp('Amumu',11,False)`=(0,0,0), `True`=(66.0,0,0) PHYS; unregistered champ identical on/off; registered champ strictly greater ON (2401.3 -> 2456.8). EhpResult gained `passive_flat_mit_phys/mag/true` (default 0.0, appended at END per the dataclass convention). ENGINE bump touched 81 assertion-pin test files (anchored quoted-literal replace only, per `feedback_engine_bump_quoted_literal_only`). 558. DONE **2026-06-21 (interactive / live practice-match diagnosis) - the in-game overlay's "dead/placeholder" symptom TRUE-root-caused to a WS mixed-content boot abort (CORRECTING item 557's backgroundThrottling diagnosis), plus three sibling overlay/coach fixes. Commits `91cf480a` + `44174fb1` + `d2170d4d` + `0a70a547`; Tier-1 (frontend + dashboard routes + rc-shell JS), no engine / no DS / no Share. CI green. Local gate: ruff clean, hygiene 13, py_compile, 44 state-builder + 8 sr_aram_worker + 253 rc-shell tests.** Operator started an SR bot practice match; the overlay showed only its pre-game placeholders despite live coach in /api/state. Root-caused live with Playwright on the EXACT overlay origin (the key move).

    **TRUE root cause - WS mixed-content boot abort (`91cf480a` + `0a70a547`).** The overlay loads over HTTPS at `https://legion-rc:8888/?overlay=1` and `web/js/main.js` opened an INSECURE `ws://legion-rc:8891/push`. Browsers BLOCK mixed-content `ws://` from an HTTPS page for any NON-localhost host (only localhost/127.0.0.1 are exempt) - the block throws a SecurityError SYNCHRONOUSLY at `new WebSocket(...)`, called unguarded at boot, which aborted the rest of init INCLUDING the HTTP /api/state poll fallback, so the page stamped body[data-shell=overlay] but never rendered. The prior backgroundThrottling diagnosis (item 557, `8c7319f5`) was a RED HERRING, masked for months because the "test in a normal browser" step used 127.0.0.1 (exempt from the block, always renders). Fix: route the WS to `ws://127.0.0.1:8891` (exempt + co-located on this 1-PC box, plain ws works) + wrap connect() so a WS failure can never abort boot. An interim `wss://` attempt was reverted - :8891 has no TLS, so wss:// spammed ssl_client_socket handshake failures (net_error -100). LESSON (memory updated): test the overlay on the legion-rc origin, never 127.0.0.1. Also added defensive Electron occlusion switches (disable-backgrounding-occluded-windows / disable-renderer-backgrounding / disable-features=CalculateNativeWinOcclusion) to rc-shell as hardening (NOT the cause).

    **SR coach gated out of Practice Tool (`91cf480a`).** `core/sr_aram_worker.py` fired the SR coach only for CLASSIC/RANKED; PRACTICETOOL was commented out, so the coach never ran in a bot practice game and the panels had no data. Re-enabled PRACTICETOOL (the coach + dispatcher already supported it). Verified: coach populated /api/state live.

    **ds-preview / ds-knobs 503 spam (`44174fb1`).** A completed 6-item build made rank_items raise / rank_for_primary_archetype return None; both routes returned 503, and the in-game BUILD panel re-requests every ~4s -> 24+ console errors per snapshot. Both routes now detect the full slot set up front and return a graceful 200 (reason=build_complete). routes_state.py uses a local `_MAX_BUILD_SLOTS=6` (NOT importing rank.DEFAULT_SLOT_COUNT - the split-brain guard forbids the in-process engine import there).

    **Em-dash from the coach reaching the HUD (`d2170d4d`).** The Haiku/Sonnet coaches emit non-ASCII punctuation into prose fields (objective etc.) which flowed to the user-facing overlay - a repo ASCII-rule violation (UI/UX audit MUST-FIX). build_state() is the single seam all four coaches pass through, so a recursive `_ascii_clean()` (translation table keyed by Unicode ORDINAL so the source stays 7-bit ASCII) maps em/en dashes, smart quotes, ellipsis to ASCII right after the coach file is read.

    **Method + open:** ran a read-only UI/UX fixture audit subagent against the live overlay (findings: this em-dash MUST-FIX done; pop-out/hierarchy SHOULD-FIX + L1 minimap timers + L3 spike cue queued). rc-shell bounced to a fresh instance (pid 4996) on the fixed main.js. NEXT: the queued UI passes + a live confirm the in-game overlay renders (operator AFK in a practice match for it). Memory [[reference_electron_overlay_throttling]] (corrected) / [[project_rc2_build]] / [[feedback_verify_before_declare_broken]].

Electron defaults `webPreferences.backgroundThrottling=true`, so once the foreground game OCCLUDES the always-on-top overlay Chromium suspends its renderer (timers ~1/s, rAF paused) and the `/api/state-stream` SSE re-render never runs - the HUD freezes on its scaffold even though the backend feeds live data, and a relaunch just re-throttles the new window. PROVEN by loading the identical `?overlay=1` page in a normal browser against the live backend mid-game: SSE delivered liveclient (Caitlyn), panes populated, QA1 glyph lit. Fix = `backgroundThrottling: false` on both overlay + companion windows; effective on next `npm start` (dev-launched, no rebuild). The diagnosis ruled out (with live probes) a stale SSE, a stale LCU phase (`lcu.phase=InProgress` confirmed), and the `renderActiveMatch` lcuPhase gate. **Awaiting operator relaunch to confirm live** - the root cause is proven so this is verification, not an open question.

    **QA4 (`f570f527`) - objective respawn-timer chips (dragon/baron/herald).** Pure frontend over the existing `liveclient.objective_events` feed; ETA = latest kill + spawn cycle (same constants as `map_state.js`). Up-now objectives brighten to the green signal. SCOPE: the "camp-timer" half is INFEASIBLE (Live Client emits no jungle-camp events); the ZoI-on-minimap add stays OPEN (needs a minimap canvas in the overlay). Render-verified in-browser with mock events (BARON UP green / DRAKE 3:00 gold); live objective data rides the next real game (bot practice had no objective kills).

    **Screen-capture finding (no code):** the vision `self_grab` is `PIL.ImageGrab` (GDI BitBlt, `vision_server/_frame.py:67`), which returns BLACK for a hardware-accelerated DirectX surface - a fullscreen/occluded game is uncapturable; only the overlay (a normal window) grabs. Pipeline itself is healthy (0.1s fresh frames). Left as an OPEN thread (operator to foreground the game / confirm Borderless; ties into QA6). **Remaining overlay OPEN:** QA6 fullscreen->Borderless hint (Win32), QA12 restructure [L], QA13 UIPI [S], QA14 display-pick [S], QA4-ZoI tail. 552. DONE **2026-06-20 (interactive) - loop-monitor terminal-flash fix. Commit `f508046e` (dashboard/routes_loop_status.py + regression test); Tier-1 (non-frozen route, no engine / no DS / no Share). CI green, 12 tests.** Operator-reported "terminal window flashing + stealing focus every ~4s, too fast to read." Root-caused (foreground-window watcher via user32 GetForegroundWindow, NOT a process sampler) to the `/loop-monitor` page (item 550): it auto-polls `/api/loop-status` every 4s, and `_last_commit()` ran `git` via `subprocess.run` with NO `creationflags`, so under pythonw.exe each poll spawned a console window that grabbed desktop focus (Windows Terminal hosting the git console). FIX = Windows-guarded `creationflags=CREATE_NO_WINDOW` (0 elsewhere), matching the dashboard/server.py vision-spawn idiom; regression test asserts the flag is passed to the git subprocess. Verified: 10 rapid /api/loop-status polls -> 0 terminal flashes (foreground stayed on the Claude app). Also recovered RC which was DOWN at session start (app pid 22040 + RC-Supervisor both exited) via `schtasks /Run /TN RC-Supervisor` (restart_trigger.txt is a no-op when the supervisor is dead, [[project_rc_supervisor_restart]]). Memory NEW [[feedback_avoid_console_flash_legion]] / [[reference_loop_monitor]].

fix = per-session git worktrees for concurrent sessions + don't co-run two heavy Opus sessions on the shared API rate limit. 530. DONE **2026-06-20 (RC 2.0 program, /RC2-Continue - Phase 4.2) - non-intrusive overlay: click-through zones + opacity + auto-hide. Commit `85d6b29e`; Tier-1 web/shell JS+CSS, NO ENGINE / 0 frozen-Python / no DS / no Share; ADR-008 asset-hash auto-reload for the renderer half (rc-shell Electron MAIN relaunch owed for the shell-side opacity/zones, same E1/P3.4/4.1 pattern). Stage 4.2 flipped DONE; banner 32 -> 33 / 62 = ~53%.** First non-DONE/CLOSED/LIVE top-to-bottom = P4.2 ("Non-intrusive overlay (click-through zones, opacity, auto-hide)"). Three named sub-features, all additive + overlay-scoped + default-safe (no shared dashboard render-path flip -> fully headless, unlike 3.3's gated pulse flip). **(1) CLICK-THROUGH ZONES:** `overlay_state.effectiveIgnoreMouse(clickThrough, zoneHover, zonesEnabled)` (pure) is the new EFFECTIVE-ignore decision layering a transient `zoneHover` + a `clickThroughZones` setting over the operator/hotkey `clickThrough` INTENT (zones OFF = legacy whole-window; ACTIVE = fully interactive; PASSIVE+zones = click-through EXCEPT over a control). New `rc-shell/src/clickthrough_zones.js` = PURE injected-string hover detector (mirrors active_indicator/drag_region; sandboxed preload cannot require modules): pointermove/pointerdown capture -> `e.target.closest(ZONE_SELECTOR)` (`#ovset, #rn-choices, #am-pane-ovds, #rc-shell-drag-region, [data-rc-zone]`) -> reports over the preload bridge (new `rcShell.setZoneHover` -> `ipcMain.on rc-shell:overlay-zone-hover`, one-way send); `window.__rcZoneHoverWired` idempotency + no-bridge guard. main.js `applyClickThrough` sets `setIgnoreMouseEvents(effective,{forward:true})` + `setFocusable(!effective)`; the ACTIVE glow still tracks the INTENT (a micro-hover must not light the full frame). Operator clicks #ovset / choices / DS knobs / drag strip WITHOUT Alt+Shift+A. Default ON. **(2) OPACITY:** `overlay_state.clampOpacity` [0.3,1.0] 2-dp; main.js `applyOverlayOpacity` -> `overlayWindow.setOpacity`; `#ovset` 30-100% range slider (HUD recedes into the game). Default 1.0 no-op. **(3) AUTO-HIDE idle recede:** new `web/js/lib/overlay_idle.js` (pure `isIdle`/`planIdle` + DOM `applyIdleAttr` + glue `initOverlayIdle`, dual ESM/CJS like condense.js) - MutationObserver on the dock mounts (same set as overlay_pulse.js) + pointer listeners reset `lastActivity`; after ~8s with no coach change AND no pointer activity the body gets `data-rc-idle="1"` and `overlay.css` section 6 dims `#view-active-match`+`main` to 0.35 (opacity-transition only, GPU-light); coach change / hover snaps back. OPACITY recede, NOT `display:none` (a hard hide would remove coaching mid-game). 529. DONE **2026-06-20 (RC 2.0 program, /RC2-Continue - Phase 4.1) - DPI + resolution-aware overlay sizing. Stage 4.1 flipped DONE; banner 31 -> 32 / 62 = ~52%.** First non-DONE/CLOSED/LIVE stage top-to-bottom = P4.1 ("Electron overlay sizing/DPI/multi-res correctness"; P4 unblocked by the 2026-06-20 Hextech greenlight). **Gap (research LIFT-C, the single highest-value sizing lift):** RC's overlay box (`rc-shell/src/overlay_state.js OVERLAY_DEFAULTS` 460x900) + dock CSS (~460px column) were a HARD 1920/100% baseline with ZERO `scaleFactor`/resolution handling - at the operator's new 2560x1440 borderless the HUD shrank to a tiny corner (the Overlay App F "content too big for the window" failure class). **FIX, pure-first (`overlay_state.js`):** `normScaleFactor` (electron#6571 guard - `getPrimaryDisplay().scaleFactor` returns a non-positive/non-number under Windows display scaling -> coerce to 1); `resolveOverlayScale(workArea)` (scale = `min` of the two axis ratios vs the 1920x1080 baseline - preserves aspect, avoids ultrawide over-scale - clamped [0.8,1.6], 2-dp; EXACTLY 1.0 at the baseline = true no-op); `resolveOverlayMetrics(display)` -> `{scale,scaleFactor,width,height}` (box grown by scale; height never exceeds the work area so the dock always fits on-screen; garbage display -> safe 1.0 box); `resolveOverlayBounds(saved, workArea, metrics?)` honors an optional scaled box (garbage/absent -> default box, backward compatible) + docks against the SCALED width; `overlayUrl(origin, panelSet, scale?)` appends `ovscale=N` ONLY when meaningfully != 1 (baseline URL byte-identical). **KEY DPI MODEL (research L3):** Electron sizes windows in DIPs and Chromium maps CSS px -> DIPs -> device px by deviceScaleFactor, so we size the DIP box against the DIP work area and DO NOT multiply by scaleFactor (that would double-apply) - scaleFactor is read only to guard + diagnose. **Wiring:** `main.js createOverlayWindow` computes metrics from `screen.getPrimaryDisplay()`, sizes the window, stores `overlayScale`, passes it to BOTH `overlayUrl` loadURL sites (create + the Alt+Shift+C panel-cycle reload). **Renderer:** `web/js/main.js` overlay-shell stamp parses `ovscale` (re-clamped [0.8,1.6] - never trust a hand-edited URL), sets `--rc-overlay-scale` on body; `web/css/overlay.css body[data-shell="overlay"]{zoom:var(--rc-overlay-scale,1)}` zooms the dock CONTENT to fill the scaled window (zoom not transform, so layout + the right-edge dock reflow; default 1 = exact baseline no-op). **TDD RED-first:** +15 node tests in `rc-shell/test/overlay_state.test.js` (normScaleFactor guard, resolveOverlayScale baseline/1440/ultrawide/clamps/garbage, resolveOverlayMetrics shape+1440+height-clamp+DPI-guard, resolveOverlayBounds metrics-override + backward-compat, overlayUrl ovscale append/omit/compose) -> RED 15 fail -> GREEN; full rc-shell node suite 80->95 in this file, 194/194 across all 8 suites. +3 real-Chromium fixture-audit tests in `tests/snapshot_panels/test_overlay_view.py` (1440 ovscale=1.3 zooms the dock to ~598px right-anchored + screenshot `overlay_sr_1440_scaled.png`; baseline-absent no-op stays ~460 with the var unset; out-of-band ovscale=2.5 ignored) -> `test_overlay_view` 14->17/17. **VERIFY (Tier-1, [[feedback_execution_efficiency_rules]] R5/R6):** rc-shell 194/194; overlay_view 17/17; sibling overlay sweep (route-smoke + priority + settings) 50/50; banned-glyph scan 0 hits across all 6 touched files. No full dual suite (Tier-1 web/shell JS cannot touch engine math). **5-phase fixture audit PASS (0 MUST-FIX, real-Chromium screenshot at 1440):** STRUCTURE right-docked single column scales cleanly (CALL/BUILD/FIGHT panes, no orphan/overflow); TYPOGRAPHY legible + proportional, no clip; HIT-TARGETS the 42/44px floors only enlarge under zoom; ASCII 0 banned glyphs (`test_no_em_dashes` PASS); HIERARCHY glance lands on the CALL headline first. **OWED (NEVER blocks):** the LIVE composited-over-League eyeball at 2560x1440 borderless (stage 4.6, operator-gated) + the rc-shell Electron MAIN relaunch -> `docs/LIVE_GAME_GATED_SYNC.md` (same operator-accepted relaunch pattern as E1/P3.4). 473. ✅ **2026-06-17 - DSP8: Enemy-comp target-preset seam - generalize the DSV3 `assume_squishy_target` binary armor assumption into four named enemy-comp presets on `rank_items_by_burst` (commit `f03dfc25`, ENGINE 1.133.0 -> 1.134.0, DEFAULT-OFF) - Tier-2 DS-engine math, DS :8893 restart + Share sync in-commit, NO frozen, NO RC restart, NO backfill (forward-only seam).** Headless gemini-loop executor cycle 10; eighth DS-permutation-swarm session (DSP8). **ROOT (plan DSP8 + the enemy-team permutation bucket):** DSV3 (item 433) added a single binary squishy-carry armor curve (`assume_squishy_target` -> `_assumed_squishy_target_armor` = 22 + 4.5/lvl, armor-only) so the burst ranker's lethality penetration had real armor to bite; but the enemy comp's actual defensive shape - a tank's stacked armor+MR, a high-CC enchanter's MR, a bruiser's mixed resists - was an unmodeled permutation, so the `/rank-assassin` item ranking valued lethality vs raw AD vs magic pen against ONLY a squishy target. **FIX (burst.py, the DSV1-4 opt-in-seam precedent):** NEW `_TARGET_PRESETS` table (`squishy` / `bruiser` / `tank` / `high_cc` -> `(armor_base, armor_per_level, mr_base, mr_per_level)`) + `_assumed_target_resists(preset, level) -> (armor, MR)` on the `base + per_level*(level-1)` curve (the DSV3 curve shape) + `_resolve_target_preset(target_preset, assume_squishy_target)`. `rank_items_by_burst` gains `target_preset` (default None -> byte-identical): when a preset is active AND the caller did not pin a positive resist, the preset's `(armor, MR)` is substituted for BOTH the baseline and every candidate, so lethality / flat pen bites a tank's armor and magic pen bites a high-CC enchanter's MR; 433. ✅ **2026-06-16 - DEEP-AUDIT cycle 36: DSV3 lethality-vs-sustained-AD burst-ranker valuation (commit `27873cc1`) - Tier-2 ENGINE 1.125.0 -> 1.126.0, DS :8893 restarted -> 1.126.0, Share re-synced (339 files) in the SAME commit.** Fifth session of the operator-directed plan refill (P6-G5 residual 3, the last DSV-* slice). **ROOT CAUSE:** `rank_items_by_burst` defaulted `target_armor=0.0`, and against zero armor `effects.effective_target_armor` floors its penetration tail at zero (`max(0.0, armor - pen_flat)`), so lethality (flat armor pen, Riot V14.1 1:1) contributed NOTHING to a ranked item's `delta_burst` - an equal-cost raw-AD item out-ranked a lethality item. This is the exact "lethality-vs-sustained tradeoff in `burst`" residual flagged by P6-G5 (ledger 424): a burst assassin's real target is a squishy CARRY with armor, against which flat pen bites; the engine's static ranker assumed a 0-armor dummy. The existing `test_lethality_helps_zed` proves `compute_burst_damage` values lethality CORRECTLY at `target_armor=80` - the gap was only in the RANKER's default target. **FIX (DEFAULT-OFF `assume_squishy_target` seam, byte-identical when off; INLINE sole orchestrator per directive - one coupled engine contract, R9 + DSV1/DSV2 precedent):** (1) `burst.py` NEW module consts `_SQUISHY_TARGET_BASE_ARMOR=22.0` / `_SQUISHY_TARGET_ARMOR_PER_LEVEL=4.5` + `_assumed_squishy_target_armor(level)` = a representative mage/ADC armor curve (22 base + 4.5 per level above 1 -> 44.5 @ L6, 67 @ L11, 98.5 @ L18; uses the engine's `clamp_level` which VALIDATES, not clamps). (2) `rank_items_by_burst` gains `assume_squishy_target: bool = False` appended at the END (dataclass/signature convention) - when True AND `target_armor <= 0.0`, a local `ranking_target_armor` is substituted into BOTH the baseline and EVERY candidate `compute_burst_damage` call (and the `BurstRankResult.target_armor` for honest surfacing) + a note; when False OR the caller pinned `target_armor>0`, `ranking_target_armor == target_armor` so the ranking is BYTE-IDENTICAL. The lethality math itself is untouched (`effective_target_armor`, ENGINE 1.5.1 V14.1 1:1) - only the ranker's target ASSUMPTION is refined. **SEAM-FIRST scope (per directive + EXCLUDED):** ships DEFAULT-OFF; the live `rank.py` scorers do NOT pass `assume_squishy_target=True` yet - flipping it ON is a separate real-game-validation-gated step (the DS Phase-D default-ON flag-flip class), so live rankings are byte-identical this cycle. **TDD:** `test_burst_squishy_target_dsv3.py` (7) red-first (ImportError on `_assumed_squishy_target_armor`) -> green: armor-curve positive/monotonic/anchor-points (22/67/98.5) + out-of-range raises (clamp_level contract); seam OFF == default == today's armor=0 ranking; seam ON sets `result.target_armor` to the assumed 67 (OFF=0); **the lethality item (Youmuu's 3142) out-values the raw-AD item (Bloodthirster 3072) MORE under the seam** via a DIFFERENCE-OF-DIFFERENCES assertion `(on_leth - on_ad) > (off_leth - off_ad)` (robust, not a fragile absolute cross-item rank - per the Testing Discipline "prefer computed quantities" rule); an explicit `target_armor=80` is NOT overridden (seam no-op). **Gate (Tier-2 dual suite):** DS-dir **7126 passed / 1 skip / 1942 subtests, exit 0**; tests/ green after the DS restart + `ds_share_sync` (the 7 pre-fix failures = 1 live-:8893 stale-version integration [`phase8_smoke` build_profile] + 6 Share doc/ingest anchors, ALL 18 green on re-run); ruff + py_compile clean; `ds_share_sync --check` in sync (339 files, engine 1.126.0). ENGINE bump touched 75 quoted-assertion pins across 67 files + the sustain-contract docstring; no historical changelog refs touched (`feedback_engine_bump_quoted_literal_only`). **Verifier subagent GATE RUN per the directive's explicit mandate (overrides R7): ALL 7 CONFIRM** (test file exists, 7 passed, ENGINE literal 1.126.0, 0 stale pins, --check in sync, full DS suite green, 18/18 drift files) - GATE PASS, 0 regressions. No frozen files touched. **NEXT (plan order): UIX1/2/3** (5-phase champ-select-SR / Home+Settings / Session+detached-PGR UI audits) - the DSV-* P6-G5 scorer-valuation residual is now fully DONE (DSV1 AP DoT-burn + DSV2 kill-state passives + DSV3 lethality-vs-sustained). Source: `docs/ORCHESTRATION_PLAN.md`.

432. ✅ **2026-06-15 - DEEP-AUDIT cycle 35: DSV2 takedown / kill-state item-passive valuation (commit `f1075c38`) - Tier-2 ENGINE 1.124.0 -> 1.125.0, DS :8893 restarted -> 1.125.0, Share re-synced (338 files) in the SAME commit.** Fourth session of the operator-directed plan refill (P6-G5 residual 2). **GAP (P6-G5, ledger 424):** the burst + auto scorers had NO seam for the on-takedown item passives, so the kill-state lethality items were under-ranked (Hubris ~#13, The Collector ~#12 live) - flagged as the "item passives needing kill/takedown state" residual. **FIX (DEFAULT-OFF kill-state OFFENSE seam, byte-identical when off; INLINE sole orchestrator - the seam is one coupled contract across 6 engine files; R9 + DSV1/OVL2 precedent):** (1) `_effects_types.ItemEffect` gains 3 fields appended at the END (dataclass convention) - `takedown_bonus_ad_base` / `takedown_bonus_ad_per_stack` (Hubris Eminence "15 (+2 per stack)" bonus AD on takedown, verbatim Meraki 16.12.1) + `execute_max_hp_pct` (The Collector Death "execute below 5% of maximum health"); all default 0.0 = byte-identical. (2) `effects.py` NEW `total_takedown_bonus_ad(effects, stacks)` + `total_execute_max_hp_pct(effects)` aggregators. (3) `dps.py` NEW module const `_ASSUMED_TAKEDOWN_STACKS=1` (operator-tunable, mirrors `ehp._TAKEDOWN_RATE_PER_FIGHT`) + an `assume_takedown` kwarg on `compute_dps` - when True folds Hubris bonus AD (15 + 2*1 = 17) into the rotation AD (`stats_for_rotation["ad"]`) + `CallContext.bonus_ad` + the per-hit display AD so AA DPS + bonus-AD procs rise; The Collector execute is DELIBERATELY NOT credited (a one-shot finisher is not sustained DPS). (4) `burst.py` `assume_takedown` on `compute_burst_damage` + `rank_items_by_burst` - threads into the internal `compute_dps` AA probe (AA per-hit picks up Hubris AD) AND the `AbilityContext.bonus_ad` (AD-ratio abilities scale off it, so Hubris raises BOTH ability and AA), and after the rune layer credits the Collector execute as a `5% * target_max_hp` TRUE-damage finisher (`execute_finisher_damage`, folded into `total_burst_damage`, needs a target max HP to size). NEW `BurstResult.takedown_bonus_ad` + `execute_finisher_damage` fields mirror the `rune_proc_damage` convention (0.0 default = byte-identical) + to_dict + notes. **KEY DESIGN GUARD (root-cause-honest):** Death's Dance carries NEITHER offense field - its takedown payoff is the Defy HEAL, already valued on the SURVIVABILITY axis (`ehp.py ItemHeal.takedown_gated`, ENGINE 1.57.0); crediting it offense damage would double-count phantom value, so the seam is a deliberate no-op for DD (a test pins DD offense byte-identical seam-on vs seam-off). **Data wired:** Hubris 6697/226697/126697 + The Collector 6676/667666/226676. **TDD:** `test_dsv2_killstate_passives.py` (16) red-first (ImportError on `_ASSUMED_TAKEDOWN_STACKS`) -> green: schema defaults, Meraki data pins (Hubris 15/2, Collector 0.05, DD none), helper math (17 AD @ 1 stack), OFF byte-identical (dps + burst + rank), Hubris DPS+burst raise (ability AND AA), Collector execute == 100 vs 2000 max HP folded into total, execute needs target HP, DD offense byte-identical. **SEAM-FIRST scope (per directive + EXCLUDED):** ships DEFAULT-OFF; the live rank scorers (`rank.py`) do NOT pass `assume_takedown=True` yet - flipping it ON is a separate real-game-validation-gated step (the DS Phase-D default-ON flag-flip class), so live rankings are byte-identical this cycle. **Gate (Tier-2 dual suite):** DS-dir **7119 passed / 1 skip / 1942 subtests, exit 0**; tests/ **7982 passed / 2 skip / 109 subtests** (the 7 pre-fix failures = 1 live-:8893 stale-version integration + 6 Share doc/ingest anchors, ALL green after the DS restart + `ds_share_sync`, re-run confirmed 18/18); ruff + py_compile clean; `ds_share_sync --check` in sync (338 files, engine 1.125.0). ENGINE bump touched 75 quoted-assertion pins; 2 historical DSV1 (1.124.0) changelog refs deliberately KEPT (`feedback_engine_bump_quoted_literal_only`). Verifier subagent SKIPPED per R7 (single-thread inline edit, no stale pipe) - fresh re-verify done instead. No frozen files touched. **NEXT (plan order): DSV3** (lethality-vs-sustained AD tradeoff in `burst.py`), then UIX1/2/3 (5-phase UI audits). Source: `docs/ORCHESTRATION_PLAN.md`.

431. ✅ **2026-06-15 - DEEP-AUDIT cycle 34: DSV1 AP damage-over-time burn valuation (commit `597ffc95`) - Tier-2 ENGINE 1.123.0 -> 1.124.0, DS :8893 restarted, Share re-synced (337 files) in the SAME commit.** Third session of the operator-directed plan refill (P6-G5 residual 1). **ROOT CAUSE:** `compute_ability_dps` (the AP / mage item scorer) mirrors `compute_dps`'s item AMP + PEN handling (ability_dps.py:950-965) but NEVER folded the item PERIODIC procs, so ability-triggered AP burn DoTs (Liandry Torment, Blackfire Baleful Blaze, Demonic Azakana) were invisible to the mage item ranking even though the auto-attack scorer has valued them via `_periodic_proc_dps` since Phase 4 - exactly the P6-G5 "AP DoT burn vs single-rotation `ability` model" gap (Veigar/Lux Liandry buried ~#23). **FIX (completed the compute_dps mirror; ROOT CAUSE: on 2026-06-03 the operator hand-edited the gist README directly on the remote (2 commits `a76cd91`+`8129ed5`, README-only), putting the remote +2 ahead of the local `~/.rc-share-gist` clone; every subsequent hook `git push origin HEAD` hit `! [rejected] (non-fast-forward)`, `_git` runs `check=True` so it raised CalledProcessError, and the git post-commit context SWALLOWED it - 45 regenerated commits accrued unpushed over 6 days with zero surfaced signal (the local mirror was correct the whole time; only the publish was blocked). FIX 1 (ops, operator-gated fork - chose "force-push lean template now" over "port the richer hand-authored README into `_README_TEMPLATE`" or "hold push"): `git push --force origin HEAD:main` -> published gist now `17ba6ab` ENGINE 1.120.0 / patch 16.12.1, fresh Share.zip (346 files), divergence cleared (`## main...origin/main`, in sync). Dropped the 2 manual-README-edit commits (reflog-recoverable; the sync regenerates README from `_README_TEMPLATE` by design, so the manual edits were always transient). FIX 2 (prevent recurrence, TDD, commit `e1fc7603`): `tools/gist_share_sync.py` (frozen=no per its arch header) - NEW `_do_push()` catches the push CalledProcessError -> `_append_log()` (git stderr + unpushed `origin/main..HEAD` count -> `logs/YYYY-MM-DD.log`) + `_write_status()` (atomic `ops/runtime/gist_sync_status.json`: `ok`/`detail`/`unpushed_commits`/`ts`) + stderr echo + returns non-zero `PUSH_FAIL_EXIT=3` (a post-commit hook can't abort the commit, but the failure is now LOGGED + health-signalled + non-zero-exited instead of silent). The no-change path also computes the unpushed backlog now -> a latent backlog is surfaced (status `ok=False` + non-zero exit) even when `Share/` is unchanged, so a false "up to date" can no longer mask a stale publish. +3 regression tests `tests/test_gist_share_sync.py` (failure path: status+log+stderr+exit code; success path: ok=True/unpushed 0; `_unpushed_count` parse + git-error safety). VERIFY (ground truth this turn): RED 3-fail -> GREEN 3-pass; ruff All checks passed; py_compile OK; hygiene trio (smart-quote/mojibake/u2500) green; live smoke `python tools/gist_share_sync.py` exit 0 + `gist_sync_status.json` = `{ok:true, detail:"up to date", unpushed_commits:0}`; zero production importers (only the post-commit hook runs it as a script, so no blast radius). **Don't-redo:** do NOT hand-edit the gist REMOTE (it diverges the remote AND the sync clobbers it from `_README_TEMPLATE` on the next push); the gist staleness health signal is `ops/runtime/gist_sync_status.json` (gitignored; `unpushed_commits`>0 or `ok=false` == stale mirror) - a future dashboard/rc_facts surface can read it. 357. ✅ **2026-06-08 - REPLAY1 operator-reported "match ingestion is not up to date after each game" - ROOT-CAUSE fix + data recovery; non-frozen `scripts/rewind_catchup.py`, ENGINE untouched 1.120.0, no Share, BACKEND+DATA only (no UI), gitignored data files not committed (gemini-headless-upgrade loop, operator "start next open items", commit `647b455e`).** Replay / rewind surface froze at 2026-05-23 while the operator kept playing. ROOT CAUSE (verified live via Riot Account-V1 + Match-V5 probes): `resolve_current_puuid` derived the account from the DB MAJORITY puuid (`operator_riot_id_from_db` -> most-frequent puuid's Riot ID -> Account-V1). After the operator switched Riot ID (SamplePlayer#Vayne -> #Trist), the DB stayed dominated by ~2900 OLD-account rows, so resolution kept re-locking the old account + never discovered the new one (chicken-and-egg: the new account has 0 DB rows yet). `lib/rewind_live_writer.py` read the stale puuid from `data/rewind_catchup.state.json` + only ever saw already-ingested old matches -> every 90s post-gameEnd live write was a silent `already_present` no-op. Probes: state puuid -> old Vayne (old URF matches, ALL already in DB); `get_account_by_riot_id("SamplePlayer","Trist")` -> the current puuid with 37 un-ingested recent matches. FIX: a persisted `riot_id` in the state sentinel is an authoritative STICKY override - `resolve_current_puuid` resolves it via Account-V1 BEFORE the DB-majority fallback (new priority step 3, ahead of step 4 DB-derived); `main()` persists `args.riot_id`. A freshly-switched account can ONLY be discovered from an explicit/persisted identity, never the majority. DATA RECOVERY (same fix, per data-fix discipline): ran `catchup --riot-id "SamplePlayer#Trist"` -> state.json corrected (puuid=rO-hF + riot_id), 37 matches backfilled, rewind DB newest 2026-05-23 -> 2026-06-08 (2904 -> 2941 rows); live writer now resolves the current account (`_resolve_latest_puuid` -> rO-hF, verified). TDD `tests/test_rewind_catchup_puuid.py` (7) pins the priority order (persisted beats DB-majority, explicit flags beat persisted, Account-V1-miss / malformed-id degrade to DB-derived). RC suite 5376 passed / 2 skip / 0 fail; ruff clean. LIVE end-to-end (next-game auto-ingest) OWED. **Don't-redo:** the DB-majority resolution was the bug - do NOT revert step 3; a future DIFFERENT-account switch needs one `catchup --riot-id NAME#TAG` run (or wiring the live LCU current-summoner Riot ID into the writer, logged FUTURE) before auto-ingest resumes. (session record `docs/ORCHESTRATION_PLAN.md` Findings 2026-06-08)

351. ✅ **2026-06-08 - parallel next-items: vision :8889 false-alarm probe fix + cdragon hard-tail CLOSED - non-frozen; ENGINE untouched 1.120.0, no Share, no DS restart, no RC restart (rc_facts is a SessionStart-hook diagnostic + ROADMAP/BACKLOG docs only).** Operator "start the next open items in parallel" -> 2 independent parallel tracks, both ground-truthed + resolved. **Track A - vision :8889 = FALSE ALARM + probe fix (`7f49499d`).** Session-start rc_facts flagged "Legion: vision server :8889 not listening" (RC-VisionServer task Running). Verify-first DISPROVED it: the server is UP - PID 2108 `moon_vision_server.py` bound `0.0.0.0:8889`, public `/health` HTTP 200 (`{alive:true, model:claude-haiku-4-5, api_key_ok:true, uptime ~6.3h}`), netsh portproxy empty. Root cause = the PROBE: `tools/rc_facts.py::_port_listening` did ONE 0.4s TCP connect and read any failure as down; the single-threaded vision server's accept() loop stalls intermittently mid frame-upload, so a fast connect times out while the port is provably LISTENING (live 5x socket probe: 3 OK at 0-15ms + 2 timeouts at ~405ms). A refused connection (truly down) raises immediately, NOT a timeout - so a timeout must NOT be read as down. Fix: `_port_listening` now retries on `socket.timeout` (3 attempts x 1.0s) and returns False immediately ONLY on a non-timeout OSError (refused). +5 tests `tests/test_rc_facts_port_probe.py` (real-listener True, closed-port False, timeout-then-success retry, all-timeouts False, refused-no-retry; a timing-assert variant was dropped - a closed Windows loopback port TIMES OUT rather than refusing, so "fast refused" is not assertable cross-platform, covered instead by the deterministic monkeypatch refused-no-retry test). Spawned follow-up chip `task_a647378a` = make the :8889 server threaded (the accept-stall ROOT cause), GATED on first confirming its handlers make no blocking inline model calls (a model client exists -> threading could allow concurrent Sonnet calls -> cost/RPM risk). **Track B - cdragon Phase-2 ratio hard-tail (NEXT-UP #1) = CLOSED (`d2ecf125`; scope + by-level drift-check, NO engine change).** A 2-agent scope+drift pass over the live 16.11.1 sidecar (`data/daemon_slayer/16.11.1/cdragon_ability_ratios.json`, 171 champs re-fetched, totals match committed `_n_fallback_blocks:577`): the 577 Meraki-fallback ratio blocks bucket as 292 emission-guard tooltip aggregates (MaxDamageTooltip / Cast2DamageMax / TotalShield - must STAY fallback by design, "fixing" them injects aggregate/duplicate damage) + ~174 needing live target/stack/branch state (buff-counter 26 / conditional-gate 1 / unknown-stat-enum 47 / EffectValue+StatBySubPart unmodeled / resource+CDR-scaled) = CLOSED-as-Meraki, the SAME live-state class as the operator-CLOSED item-232 conditional-target-state arc + 111 by-level (per-champ-level arrays: ByCharLevelBreakpoints linear+stepped / ByCharLevelInterpolation / bare Breakpoint). Only the by-level bucket is offline-resolvable, so it was drift-checked vs the frozen Meraki the engine consumes (`champion_abilities.json damage_blocks`): VERDICT ALIGNED, not stale. Decisive finding - cdragon by-level indexes by CHAMP LEVEL 1..18 while Meraki indexes by SPELL RANK 1..5 (different axes of the same growth, which is WHY the per-rank Meraki schema cannot represent them and the resolver falls them back); the 107 evaluable blocks are overwhelmingly auxiliary (caps / minion+monster mods / heal+shield / durations / move-speed / resist-grants / modifiers whose primary damage is already a sibling MECHANICAL calc the engine reads), and the genuine same-quantity pairs AGREE (Zeri Q 17.06 vs 17.14 = 0.5%, Vladimir Q 110 vs 111 = 0.9%, AurelionSol E 150 vs 150 = 0.0%, Pantheon Q 123.5 vs 115 = 7.4%; the apparent "65/80 drift >10%" was spurious cross-mechanic pairing - flat caps / 0-modifiers / heals / durations mis-paired to a damage _pct). Re-sourcing would need a NEW champ-level-indexed schema axis (not a value swap) for near-zero gain = churn -> by-level DEFERRED to BACKLOG (only if a future surface needs a champ-level-indexed ratio axis). Operator chose drift-check-first from a framed 3-way (build-now / close-all / drift-check) -> the data drove DEFER. **Verify:** local gate GREEN this turn - `ruff check .` all-passed, py_compile (rc_facts + test) OK, hygiene trio + 5 probe tests = 17 passed; CI success on BOTH d2ecf125 + 7f49499d (gh run 27115133400 / 27113939131). Scratch evidence (census + drift rows + probe scripts) under `docs/_scratch/` (gitignored). **Don't-redo:** cdragon hard-tail is CLOSED - do NOT re-pitch a calc-graph resolver (conditional/buff-counter/target-HP = live-state, Meraki-permanent; by-level = aligned-not-stale, a different axis not a stale value); remaining cdragon GROWTH needs a NEW extractor key, not a resolver (memory `reference_ds_forward_marker_exhausted`). 350. ✅ **2026-06-07 - RC-GeminiAudit nightly task fix (`tools/gemini_audit.ps1`) - exit-code-masking bug + diagnostics + model fallback; non-frozen; no engine / Share / DS / RC touch (the script + its outputs are gitignored advisory state).** Session-start anomaly flagged `RC-GeminiAudit` LastTaskResult=1 (the nightly gemini read-only auditor). Root-caused 2 layers + verified the external cause live. **(1) Code bug (FIXED):** the runner used `Write-Error "..."; exit N` for its 2 failure paths (missing key -> 2; gemini empty after retries -> 3), but the surrounding `$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'` makes `Write-Error` TERMINATING - it threw a WriteErrorException BEFORE reaching `exit N`, so PowerShell exited a bare **1** with NOTHING logged (the gemini_audit.log success-write is the last line, never reached on failure). The task showed an undiagnosable exit 1 (no log line for the 06-07 03:00 run). Replaced both sites with a NEW `Fail($msg,$code)` helper: appends a `FAIL code=N <reason>` line to `logs/gemini_audit.log` + `Write-Warning` (never terminating, WarningPreference not ErrorActionPreference) + `exit $code` - so failures now carry the INTENDED code + a logged reason. **(2) Resilience (ADDED):** new `Invoke-GeminiAudit($model,$tries)` helper + a model FALLBACK - the primary `RC_GEMINI_MODEL` (operator-set `gemini-3-pro-preview`, which WORKED through 06-06) is tried 3x; on persistent empty it falls back to `gemini-2.5-flash` (override via `RC_GEMINI_FALLBACK_MODEL`) for 2 more tries before Fail(); the output header + log now record the model ACTUALLY used (`$usedModel`), so a preview-model-specific throttle no longer kills the advisory audit. **(3) External root cause (verified live, NOT code-fixable):** the key is VALID - `GET v1beta/models?key=` lists 50 models - but `generateContent` returns **429 Too Many Requests** for `gemini-2.5-flash` (quota / RPM exhausted; the memory notes free-tier quota is shared with Google Antigravity). The 429 self-recovers on quota reset; my back-to-back repro/probe calls (~10 in 20 min) also added RPM load the once-nightly cadence does not. **Verify:** PowerShell `Parser::ParseFile` clean + 0 non-ASCII bytes (CLAUDE.md PS-double-quoted-string mojibake guard); a live background run exercised + logged BOTH the fallback and the Fail() path (exit 3); the audit outputs (`docs/EXTERNAL_REVIEW_*.md` + `ops/runtime/gemini_last_audit.txt` marker + `logs/gemini_audit.log`) are all gitignored -> the ONLY tracked change is `tools/gemini_audit.ps1`. Deliberately did NOT re-run the scheduled task (it would re-hit the same 429 -> exit 3; it goes green on the next cadence once quota frees - do not hammer it). Memory `project_gemini_auditor` updated. **Don't-redo:** the exit-code masking is FIXED - a future nonzero RC-GeminiAudit now logs WHY to `logs/gemini_audit.log` (read that FAIL line FIRST). A genuine CS->game flip still promotes via the ungated GameStart/InProgress phase arms, so no real promotion is lost. +5 tests (`ChampSelectNullBlipLiveGateTests`, RED->GREEN); view-router 38 passed, mode-flap-dom 12 passed; node --check + py_compile clean. Applied behavior UNCHANGED (display-only).
